20260612 艦長QA重點紀要
核心讀者：基金經理人、產業分析師 | 發布日期：2026年6月12日
文件概述： 本報告針對2026年6月12日舉行之大型演講問答（QA）環節進行深度內容摘要，旨在為機構投資人
（Portfolio  Managers）與科技產業分析師（Analysts）提供關鍵的供應鏈技術轉移、客戶動態及產能預估。
本次QA核心聚焦於大客戶（Google等）超大型架構與AI晶片封裝設計的變更、晶片外掛光學元件（OEO/
CPO）時程、各家ASIC與CPU晶片架構競爭、以及先進封裝（E-interconnect/CoWoS）的良率瓶頸與潛在商
機。
一、 AI架構與晶片外掛光學（CPO/OEO）時程
800G與超大型Scale-Out網路時程： 業內高度關注的NVL76/76與下一代超大集群架構。明（2027）年搭
載NVIDIA新平台的量產趨勢明確，且後（2028）年起將大幅擴展。
CPO / OEO（全光網路）切換節點： 市場傳言CPO可能於2029年導入。艦長指出，若2029年將OEO封裝直

接整合或大舉大宗導入GPU旁，確實符合全光網路發展進程。然而，短中期內（2028年左右），在
48V Ready的電力架構下，由於高速訊號傳輸無法再使用傳統的PCB板，因此訊號從處理器拉出後，將全
面走向光通訊或專屬高速連接結構。
Spectrum-6交換機與客戶動態： Broadcom（博通）主導的Spectrum-6交換器方案預計於明（2027）年
開始在大型雲端客戶（如Google）進行數萬台規模的初期量產與測試。
二、 大客戶ASIC與主要Foundry/Service供應商競爭格局
1. Google TPU B9 / V9 與 V10 / B10 晶片佈局
TPU  B9（現行架構）： 目前核心由Marvell（美滿電子）提供Service。市場傳言Google意圖提升聯發科
（MediaTek）的供貨比重以降低對博通（Broadcom）與美滿的依賴。艦長評論，將ASIC代工百分之百移
轉為極為劇烈的供應鏈結構轉變，背後通常牽涉到非技術性的特定商業考量，而非僅為單純的分散供應商風

險。
博通（Broadcom）的關鍵角色： 博通並非毫無反制產品。其目前研發的八通道處理器方案（Sunfish
Ultra），本質上是將兩顆晶片封裝在一起（業界稱為Whalefish）。目前該款晶片定位更偏向Anthropic等
特定大模型客戶。若Google自研的TPU由聯發科主導，則外賣形式的Rack方案則預計繼續交由博通處理
（因博通具備較完整的CPO、光通訊及網絡交換器Rack整合能力）。
TPU  V10  /  B10（下一代架構）： 供應鏈目前觀察到，V10世代方案代號為Icefish，目前全數由聯發科
（MediaTek）主導，此代產品線中暫未看到博通的產品身影。
博通的長線替代晶片（Mirage）： 博通另有一顆代號為Mirage的TPU-like產品線，預計於2029年量產。不
過此晶片目前的定位與市場接受度較為特殊，與Google的核心合作量能尚未放大，主要仍以Sunfish家族系
列的光通訊Rack整合為大宗。
2. 美滿電子（Marvell）與其他周邊晶片商角色

美滿電子（Marvell）在Google  TPU生態系中主要提供周邊搭配方案（如CXL  Controller等），並預計在
V10世代提供類似LPU（Language Processing Unit）的周邊晶片支持，而非主導TPU核心晶片。

### Page 2

三、 先進封裝良率瓶頸、技術變革（E-interconnect vs CoWoS）
【核心投資爭點】客戶是否會因封裝瓶頸離開台積電？
艦長給予明確且強烈的投資指引：絕對沒有任何主要客戶能真正離開台積電。 Google、博通等雲端或晶片大
廠並非傳統半導體大廠，對半導體先進製程與封裝技術缺乏長期的底層信賴與自主研發根基。非台積電陣營
（如Intel、Samsung）在先進製程與封裝的踩雷（技術故障）機率極高，大客戶在多次嘗試並遭遇挫折後，
最終仍會回歸台積電先進封裝生態系。博通之前曾計畫將晶片全數轉往Intel代工，但最終受限於技術成熟度，
仍須依賴台積電。
1. E-interconnect（Intel 方案）與良率挑戰
市場普遍關注大客戶下一代晶片因Die Size（晶粒尺寸）過大，導致傳統CoWoS封裝出現Warping（翹曲）
問題。現行嘗試的解決方案包括將Carrier更換為玻璃材質（Glass  Substrate），藉以提升平整度並降低翹
曲率。
Intel力推的E-interconnect封裝技術，雖然技術概念先進，但其最大的技術難點在於Mix-Bump（混合凸

塊）製程。由於主晶片通常需要融合兩種不同Foundry（代工廠）的製程技術，必須同時在基板上處理
Micro-Bump（微凸塊）與Standard-Bump（標準凸塊）。此類複雜凸塊製程在應力與焊接良率上面臨極
大挑戰，目前仍处于研發與小規模測試階段。
2. 產能與良率的連鎖效應（對NVIDIA之利弊）
若大客戶的晶片因先進封裝良率無法突破而導致出貨延遲，這對NVIDIA（輝達）而言將是最大的利多。
由於NVIDIA目前在整個供應鏈中具備極強的產能綁定與控制力（包括在CoWoS產能、先進散熱、高頻寬記
憶體HBM等周邊料件上皆已與一線供應商提早簽訂排他或優先供應協議），若美系雲端大廠自研ASIC受
阻，市場AI算力需求將全數倒向NVIDIA方案。
四、 CPU市場發展與CSP自主架構趨勢
ARM 架構全面超越 X86 趨勢明確： 艦長預估，在超大型資料中心（Hyperscale Data Centers）中，ARM
架構CPU的市佔率在未來數年內將正式超越  50%，壓過傳統X86架構。Intel雖然開始為CSP代工伺服器處理

器，但進度落後且難以逆轉大局。
四大CSP（雲端服務供應商）自主CPU佈局：
Amazon：具備成熟度最高的Graviton系列處理器。
Google：全面推動自研Axion CPU。
Microsoft：推出自研Cobalt CPU。
Meta：與SocialNexus合作開發的自研CPU方案亦預計於今年（2026年）開始陸續開出規模。
第三方ARM處理器晶片商： 在四大CSP自研CPU之外，市場主要由NVIDIA的Grace  CPU、博通、以及高通
（Qualcomm）等業者積極搶進。ARM官方自行推出的微架構方案，由於商業模式限制，在資料中心CPU
的直接市佔率反而不及上述晶片大廠。

### Page 3

五、 電力與超高壓供電架構（800V）對機櫃設計的影響
800V 高壓供電的必然性： 隨著AI機櫃總體功耗從過去的120kW一路上升至下一代架構的200kW+，傳統
的低壓高電流供電模式會面臨嚴重的線路損耗（I²R  Loss）。因此，機櫃內部或資料中心端直接導入800V高
壓供電架構，配合HD-VPC（高密度垂直供電）已成為產業必經之路。
HD-VPC 與 800V 導入時程： 供應鏈原先預期，800V高壓系統與超高功率電力機櫃（HD-VPC）要到下一代
更遙遠的架構才會全面普及。然而根據最新調研，大客戶與NVIDIA在最新的先進機櫃設計（如B200/
Ultra、甚至是後續的Rubin平台周邊）中，已提早將800V與高壓電力架構（如54V轉高壓等方案）列為積極
導入項目。這將加速電源管理晶片（PMIC）、高壓MOSFET、及變壓元件供應鏈的產值提升。
【分析師參考】2026-2029 關鍵晶片及技術進程預估表

應鏈變動風險。

南亞科 (2408 TT)  Trading Buy

系統性風險，調降至 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： Kioxia(258A JP) 、 SanDisk(SNDK US) 、 Solidigm 及
Cisco(CSCO US) 認購南亞科私募普通股，對南亞科而言，此為
具策略意義的合作，除挹注資金外，亦有助於綁定長期客戶需
求，並提升拓展未來 CSP 等大客戶的機會。供給端方面， DRAM
大廠持續將資本支出與產能轉向 HBM 與先進製程 DDR5，壓縮
傳統 DDR4 與 LPDDR4 供給；需求端受惠 AI 伺服器與邊緣運
算帶動記憶體搭載量提升，支撐整體 DRAM 報價維持高檔。在
價格上行帶動下，預估南亞科 2026 年稅後 EPS 達 54.47 元。過
去 3 個月記憶體產業面臨多項利空雜音，包括現貨價格波動、
中國供給擴張疑慮、終端需求放緩，及近期市場對 NVIDIA Vera
平台 SOCAMM2 配置調整的擔憂。然而， DRAM 及 NAND 產品
報價持續上漲，顯示產業供需結構及基本面未出現明顯轉弱跡
象。考量短期市場波動加劇，因此將投資評等調降至 Trading 
Buy ，待市場情緒及籌碼結構趨穩後再重新檢視投資機會。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元：1Q26 營收為 490.87 億元 ，QoQ+63.11% ，
南亞科 DRAM 平均銷售價格較 4Q25 增加 ，ASP 約 QoQ+>70% 。
匯率對營收影響 QoQ+1~+3% ，Shipment QoQ-4~-6% 。毛利率受
Blend ASP 上升與匯率有利影響 ， 1Q26 毛利率從 4Q25 的 49.04%
上揚至 67.87% ，增加 18.83 個百分點。 1Q26 稅後 EPS 7.55 元，
淨值 62.25 元。  
2026 年資本支出提升至 520 億元 ：南亞科董事會通過 2026 年
的資本支出預算以不超過新台幣 520 億元為上限， YoY+287% ，
其中 62 億元是 2025 年遞延至 2026 年，資本支出中 70% 用在新
廠的廠務無塵室等，剩下 30% 則會使用在設備採購上。預估新
廠將在 2027 年初裝機， 1H27 正式量產，規劃是 1H28 可以達到
月產能 15~20K 片 /月的目標，以 DDR5 為主。目前 DDR5 占南
亞科約 10% ，公司目標是維持在 10% 以上並逐步提升，但仍需
視整體缺貨結構而定。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 8 日

2 群益投顧 
  南亞科 ESG ：  
佈局綠色科技生產  
DRAM 為智慧世代的關鍵零組件，南亞科研發先進且具高效能的 「環境友善
產品」以符合市場與客戶需求。所有產品於設計初期時，即導入生命週期思
維與綠色設計，透過綠色產品推動委員會進行綠色產品管理。

重視人才發展與培養  
「人才」是公司創新、營運與永續發展的根本，強化公司全方位人才培育與
發展，南亞科技與長庚大學簽署成立記憶體學程碩士班，學生除可獲高額獎
學金，更可獲得畢業後的就業機會。南亞科技期盼從育才、用才、引才、聚
才、留才各方面，強化臺灣記憶體產業在國際上的競爭力。

共同攜手永續夥伴  
南亞科攜手上下游合作夥伴一同前行， 295 家供應商認同公司的永續理念，
一同簽署南亞科技企業社會責任承諾書且簽署率達 100% 、輔導供應商進行
永續改善，稽核、輔導與改善率均 100% 完成，並持續辦理供應鏈研討會。
此外，也與供應商共同合作推動人權及環境相關的議題。

永續績效備受肯定  
南亞科視 ESG 為企業的重要承諾，南亞科追求經營績效的同時，也同步落
實 ESG 的平衡發展。南亞科技參加 2025 第 18 屆台灣企業永續獎評選，公
司已連續第九年獲頒多項殊榮，獲獎包括年度最佳報告 (製造業 )、台灣 100
大永續典範企業獎及四項永續單項績效  – 氣候領袖獎、創新成長領袖獎、
資訊安全領袖獎與透明誠信領袖獎。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

創見 (2451 TT)  Trading Buy

後續毛利率擴張有限 ， 下調至Trading Buy ，目 標 價393 元。 
投資建議 ： AI 需求佳，大幅提升記憶體需求，目前原廠供應的
合約滿足率低於 50% ，創見雖然也與原廠簽訂長期供貨合約
（ LTA ） ，但原廠仍是依照既定配額供貨，創見目前庫存水位約
100 多億元， 2Q26 庫存金額還會增加，但主要是單價上升，實
際數量變化不大。目前 DRAM 與 Flash 供給都相當吃緊，雖 3
大原廠已投入大量資本支出擴產，但最快也要到 2027 年才陸續
開出新產能，推升報價進一步上揚，預估創見 2026 年稅後 EPS 
106.10 元。創見公告 05/2026 合併營收達 62.98 億元， MoM-
15.46% 、YoY+469.83% ；累計 04/2026~05/2026 營收達 137.5 億
元，高於 1Q26 整季營收 136.3 億元。記憶體報價仍處上升趨
勢，支撐獲利成長，惟隨市場預期價格漲勢趨緩，毛利率擴張
空間有限，因此下調至 Trading Buy ，目標價 393 元。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 18.86 元：因 AI 需求爆發引發結構性缺貨，記
憶體供不應求，在工控市場剛性需求帶動下，創見 1Q26 營收為
136.28 億元， QoQ+100.31% ，毛利率  76.39% ，季增 14.77 個百
分點，稅後純益  81.22 億元， QoQ+148.95% ，稅後 EPS 18.86
元。毛利率高於同業主因在於自動化與生產效能優勢，及低成
本結構，包括許多廠房已使用 20、 30 年，折舊接近完成，全球
據點也多為自有廠房，因此整體營運成本低於同業。  
創見工業用佔比 70% 以上： 創見 2026 年初工控相關應用營收
佔比已升至  70% 以上， 2Q26 占營收比重增加至 90% ，主要是
價格高，消費性通路銷售明顯下滑。創見工控以高階嵌入式市
場為主，涵蓋工業用 SSD 、 Flash 、記憶卡與  DRAM 模組，廣
泛應用於 AIoT 、醫療、交通及強固型電腦。創見在記憶體上游
原廠宣布產品 EOL 之前，已提前 2 年鎖定採購量與長約配額，
讓公司在市場面臨 DRAM 和 NAND Flash 舊世代缺料潮時，能
確保穩定的出貨能力。因工控產品需長期穩定供貨，且為客製
化需求，毛利率較高，是創見撐住缺貨潮、達成高獲利的主力。

2 群益投顧 
  創見 ESG ：  
創見官網將  ESG 分別對應環境 （永續環境） 、社會 （社會公益） 與治理 （公
司治理） ，並於 2025 年成立永續發展委員會來推動，涵蓋政策規劃、成效
檢視與跨部門執行。  近期成效包括修訂 《永續發展實務守則》 、納入  TCFD 
氣候風險評估，以及持續發行年度永續報告書。  
E：環境  
創見強調廢棄物管理、節能措施、有害物質審查與低碳轉型。官網寫到公司
投入環保產品開發，並響應氣候變遷規畫減碳政策，透過評估風險與機會來
降低能源消耗與碳排放。  
S：社會  
社會面聚焦維護社會公益與利害關係人權益。官網依《永續發展實務守則》
承諾善盡責任，透過永續發展委員會的社會公益小組推動相關活動。  
G：治理  
治理面由行政副總經理任永續負責人，設立公司治理小組處理政策與資訊
揭露。官網強調董事會監督、跨部門協作，並將  ESG 納入管理方針。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

義隆(2458)

買進 
Price(06/08):156.5

上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受 NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
義隆(2458)成立於 1994 年，為國內晶圓設計廠，產品應用聚焦於
NB 且多為全球領域龍頭 ， 觸控板及觸控螢幕晶片及Point Stick 市
佔率高達 50%以上，2025 年產品組合包含觸控板 46%、觸控螢幕
晶片 22%、 指紋辨識12%、Point Stick 13%、MCU 2%及其他 3%。
銷售地區則為香港 73%、中國 18%、台灣 7%、其他 2%，競爭對
手主要為 Synaptics。

日 週 月 ⚫ 近況更新 
客戶急單挹注，26Q1 淡季不淡 
義隆 26Q1 營收 32.2 億元 ，Q+12.8%，Y+3.4%， 毛利率47.63%，
稅後淨利 7.1 億元，Q-4.2%，Y+28.6%，EPS 2.46 元。零組件的
短缺和記憶體成本的上漲，讓客戶加急下單， 營收表現呈現淡季不
淡。然而產品組合的變化也讓整體毛利率略為下滑，費用則有部分
政府的補助讓整體本業獲利較上季度成長。

AI 產品貢獻逾雙位數，產品組合有望轉佳 
義隆 5 月營收 10.0 億元，M-12.0%，Y+3.6%，4 月營收 11.4 億
元，M-6.7%，Y+6.3%，4-5 月合計 21.5 億元，Y+5.0%。客戶端
持續增加零組件的庫存備貨，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的營收貢獻也
逐步拉高，4-5 月合計貢獻營收 11%，因此公司展望 26Q2 營收維
持 32-34 億元水準 ， 毛利率則預期產品組合相對改善下 ， 預估區間
為 46.5-49.5%，然而光罩費用及展覽等支出也讓整體費用水準提
升，因此預估 OPM 區間為 21.4-25.9%。綜合以上，研究部預估
義隆 26Q2 營收 33.4 億元 ，Q+3.5%，Y+10.1%， 毛利率48.13%，
稅後淨利 6.7 億元，Q-4.6%，Y+90.1%，EPS 2.21 元。

無人機產品發酵，切入矽光子領域 
自 25Q3 起義隆 AI 影像辨識產品已開始正式量產出貨，營收貢獻
也在上半年提升至雙位數占比，除了車用 ADAS 產品外，無人機
的 ISP 晶片貢獻也將跟隨與無人機廠雷虎及其他合作夥伴出貨增
加， 此外義隆產品也將從單晶片轉往 Camera 模組甚至整個通訊模
組，產品單價將為數倍成長，年底更有望開始往三光鏡頭酬載產品
發展 ， 產品單價又將會再往另一水準躍升 ， 另外地面飛控站( GCS )
也會從面板模組到含指紋辨識、陣列天線等整組方案出貨，期望在
非紅供應鏈趨勢下，全球年百萬架的無人機需求帶動出貨的增長。
-1.26 -6.29 3.3

另外義隆也將切往矽光領域，透過投資 PETA Optroni cs，義隆將
開始研發下一代 3.2T EIC 產品，目前PETA Optronics 已有 800G
及 1.6T PIC 產品，因此研究部正向看待公司在未來透過與 PIC 設
計公司的合作共同推出 OE 產品來切入矽光子領域 。 至於在NB 產
品方面，由於上半年客戶的急單拉貨，使營收表現呈現淡季不淡，
然而公司同時也時刻觀察客戶庫存水準來調整出貨節奏 ， 以免重回
先前 Overbooking 的情況，另外由於上游代工和封測廠成本的上
漲，公司預計將於 7 月開始漲價，同樣有助於產品獲利率的提升。
綜合以上，研究部預估義隆 2026 年營收 135.4 億元，Y+9.8%，
毛利率 48.02%，稅後淨利 28.9 億元，Y+18.2%，EPS 9.50 元。

結論 
上半年客戶的急單讓營收呈現淡季不淡，同時 AI 影像辨識產品的
貢獻也逐步發酵，26Q2 已來到雙位數水準，預計隨著無人機產品
從單晶片轉往整體模組方案 ， 倍數成長的單價將進一步帶動營收的
增長。此外義隆也透過投資 PIC 設計公司 PETA 來切入矽光子領
域，期望共同開發出下一代 3.2T EIC 產品。綜合以上，研究部預
估義隆 2026 年 EPS 9.50 元，目前 PE 為 16.4x，參考歷史 PE 區
間為 12-24x，考量公司開始往無人機、矽光子領域布局，前者將
開始貢獻營收，後者則會逐步發表新品，讓義隆擺脫過往受NB 景
氣波動的影響，因此給予 22xPER，目標價 210 元，建議買進。

800V HVDC: From Server Power Upgrade to Full AI Factory Architecture Shift  —
On the power system, the shift to 800V HVDC is not just a PSU upgrade, but a full
AI rack architecture change. Traditional AC systems require multiple conversion
stages from UPS, distribution, server PSU and low-voltage DC delivery, causing
power loss, heat, copper weight and cabling complexity. 800V HVDC reduces

conversion steps and brings high-voltage DC closer to compute racks, supporting
future rack density from around 100kW toward 500kW–1MW. This changes the
supplier’s opportunity. Busbar, cable and connector vendors benefit from higher
demand for compact high-voltage DC busbars, 125A+ connectors, hot-swap
design, insulation, arcing protection and rack-level safety. PSU suppliers move
from server PSU modules toward centralized power shelves and power racks

integrating rectifiers, DC/DC conversion, BBU, monitoring and protection. Cooling
also becomes more critical: higher rack power makes liquid cooling mandatory,
driving CDU, cold plates, manifolds, leak detection and quick-disconnect demand.
Overall, 800V HVDC turns AI infrastructure from server assembly into power-
cooling-compute system integration, favoring suppliers with system design,
reliability and safety certification capability. We are positive on Hon Hai, Delta and

AVC.
ASPEED: From BMC to Server-Control Platform —  Global No. 1 BMC maker
ASPEED also demonstrates its product roadmap and is moving from a traditional
BMC upgrade cycle toward a broader server-control platform for AI servers. The
core BMC transition is AST2700/2750, which starts ramping in 2H26, followed by
AST2800, targeted for mass production in 2028. AST2800 carries roughly 2x the
cost of AST2700, implying higher ASP , specifications, more advanced process

adoption and stronger content per server. Beyond BMC, ASPEED is expanding into
SMC, PRo T, I/O expander and eFPGA SoC products. AST1040, the next-generation
SMC, is expected to be production-ready in 1H27, while AST1840, the eFPGA SoC,
should ramp in 2H27. The company also plans to integrate Caliptra into new
generations of all chips, highlighting the rising importance of server security,
platform root-of-trust and remote-management functions. The bigger trend is

that AI servers require more telemetry, security, control and firmware-level
coordination, making BMC/SMC less like low-value support chips and more like
essential infrastructure controllers.  T ogether with various racks including existing
NVL72 and ASIC business, the new CPU, LPU, storage and SPX racks from Nvidia,
we expect strong BMC growth in the longer term to persist.

Asia Vital Components
(3017.TW; NT$2700.0; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$3,450 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,

rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-
plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not

been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Delta Electronics
(2308.TW; NT$2360.0; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$2,480 is based on 40x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since

2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with
customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly

than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.
Hon Hai Precision
(2317.TW; NT$301.5; 1; 02 Jun 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price of NT$360 is based on 20x 2026E EPS, which is at the higher
end of the historical trading range. We believe this multiple is justified by the
company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new

03 June 2026 Citi Research
6
endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been
able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus we believe a P/E
valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand; 2) intensified peer
competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress;

and 4) higher-than-expected impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C., Taipei
Branch
Lotes (3533.TW): Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day: Server remains the
key growth driver , despite ongoing material cost & supply risk
3 June 2026 | 6:52PM CST
We hosted meetings with Lotes at our Taiwan Computex & Corporate Day (June
2nd). Key takeaways include:
(1) Management expects CPU-related product shipments to grow ~20% YoY in 2026,
after factoring in potential material/component supply shortage, while key

shortages include CPU, memory, PCB, and passive components.
(2) Lotes continues to make progress in new AI-related products and expects to
begin shipping SoCAMM in 3Q26 (Lotes is the first supplier for Vera Rubin), and also
expects NPO products to start contributing in 2027. Management expects AI-related
products to account for 2-3% of total revenue in 2026, with contribution expanding
to double-digit over the next 3-5 years.
(3) Lotes expect raw material cost pressure on GM to persist into 2Q26. However,

management expects GM to improve in 2H26, driven by the release of new AMD
Venice CPU, which will see ASP increase of at least 15% vs. previous generation (23%
increase in pin count vs. AMD’s SP5 socket).
(4) The company continues to engage in price negotiations with clients, while pricing
for AMD Venice has already incorporated higher material costs. Management
mentioned the base case is no price down for the existing and new products (vs.
typical industry practice with price erosion of ~5% in year 1, ~3% in year 2 and ~1%

in year 3 after new product release).
(5) For the CPU platform, Lotes mentioned Eagle Stream penetration increased from
75-80% in 1Q26 to current 80-85%, while Birch Stream should further increase to
20-30% by end of 2026 from the current 15%. For AMD, Venice should account for
15-20% (revise down from 20-25%) of total servers by the end of 2026 but with
majority of contribution from high-end solution (SP7 with 7536 pins) and expects to
see contribution from the low-end solution (SP8 with 5572 pins) starting in 2027.

Investment view
We maintain our relative cautious view on Lotes’ business outlook, considering (1)
the rising material cost environment might sustain and the company has adopted a
stable pricing rather than cost pass through via price increase, given the risk of losing
market share and (2) limited contribution from new AI-related products (including
SoCAMM, QD, GPU socket and CPO/NPO socket). We continue to see strong CPU
server demand in near term and extend to coming years (see AMD report here

www.gs.com/research/hedge.html

which we believe will be a key growth driver for Lotes in near-to-mid term; however, we
continue to see risk of rising material costs, which should offset the margin expansion
driven by new CPU socket products.
Lotes is a connector supplier mainly focusing on the niche CPU socket market with more
than 40% share in 2024, but we see pressure for its market share outlook in the coming
years considering the intensifying competition in the server CPU market. We maintain

our positive view on the company’s GM expansion potential longer term, which we
believe will be mainly driven by better product mix (Venice server CPU to be released in
2H26 and Oak Stream server CPU to be released in 2027, both with better ASP and
margin). Meanwhile, Lotes’ CPU socket product line’s ASP/GM are higher than the
company average, and we see scope for both to grow even faster in the new product
generation given the larger socket size and pin count required. However, the company’s

new product line, Quick Disconnects (UQD or MQD) for liquid cooling systems, is likely to
deliver very limited revenue/gross profit c
ontribution in the coming years (0.4/1.0%
revenue and 0.2/0.7% GP contribution in 2026E/2027E) given thermal solution
providers typically ship cold plates as integrated modules and leading thermal suppliers
tend to secure QD supply in-house or through affilia
ted suppliers. We are Neutral rated
on Lotes considering the share loss risk in server sockets and the shares trading at the

historical peak P/E level, making valuation less attractive, despite the company’s solid
revenue/GM expansion trend driven by socket upgrading demand. Key risks: (1)
stronger-/weaker-than-expected server shipment growth which would raise/lower
server CPU socket demand; (2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs without
product pricing adjustment; (3) slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related
contribution
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$1,790 is based on a target

P/E of 17.5x (+0.5 SD above company’s average valuation since 2020) applied to our
2H26E-1H27E EPS.
Key upside/downside risks: (1) Stronger-/weaker-than-expected server CPU demand,
(2) lower-/higher-than-expected component costs, and (3)
slower-/faster-than-expected AI server-related contribution

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

## Document 082: 260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware

Source PDF: all report pdf/260604_daiwa_Taiwan Datacentre Hardware.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-06-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: During Computex 2026, we arranged booth tours for our investors with 
representatives of companies within our coverage, including Wiwynn, Aspeed, Delta, Hon 
Hai, and Chenbro, alongside several other companies. In conjunction with keynote 
addresses delivered by leading global technology companies, we believe the primary focus 
of Computex 2026 remained centered on the CPU domain - specifically, the role of CPUs 
in orchestrating the broader ecosystem for agentic AI, as well as the emerging total 
addressable market (TAM) arising from AI PCs incorporating NVIDIA's Spark chipset. 
Across the booths we visited, the majority of vendors showcased components and 
solutions applicable to NVIDIA's Vera Rubin generation platform. These demonstrations 
should be regarded as indicative of trial production activity within the Taiwan supply chain 
ahead of the official volume ramp, which is anticipated to commence in 4Q26, with the 
underlying concepts having been primarily introduced at GTC. With respect to key 
technology transitions, the developments that garnered the most attention at the 
conference included CPO-based AI server architecture, Co-Packaging Connectors (CPC), 
High-Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) power delivery, and microgrid solutions. In conclusion, 
we encourage investors to maintain a sustained focus on CPU-related investment themes, 
as well as on specification upgrades driven primarily by escalating high-speed 
transmission requirements. Our key Buy ideas include: Aspeed, Lotes (3533 TT, 
TWD2,400), Wiwynn, Delta, TUC (6274 TT, TWD1,695), EMC (2383 TT, TWD4,830), and 
Accton (2345 TT, TWD2,510).

Wiwynn (6669 TT, TWD5,570, Buy [1]) 
• According to investor feedback, Wiwynn's booth was widely regarded as one of the 
most compelling exhibitions at Computex, owing to its demonstration of a Co-Packaged 
Optics (CPO) computing rack. The demonstration was developed in collaboration with 
several key partners, including Ayar Labs - contributing the optical engine and External 
Laser Small Form Factor Pluggable (ELSFP) technology - and GUC (3443 TT 
TWD4,400, Hold [3], covered by Rick Hsu), contributing the XPU. It is our understanding 
that the demonstration remains under development, and the collaboration with Ayar 
Labs is currently in a pilot stage. Wiwynn's objective is to ensure that it acquires the 
requisite capabilities and expertise ahead of CPO technology reaching broader 
commercial maturity. Based on our observations, Wiwynn's focus appears to be 
concentrated on computing tray development, while the scale-out and scale-up switch 
racks are expected to be contributed by other partners. 
• Another notable highlight from Wiwynn's exhibition was its demonstration of AMD's 
Helios platform. Based on our understanding, AMD's Helios architecture would comprise 
18 computing units, each configured with four GPUs and one CPU per rack. Given the 
physical dimensions of AMD's MI450 series GPU and connectivity speed requirements, 
the GPU layout is positioned at the bottom of the PCB in order to optimize connectivity 
with the scale-up system. According to Wiwynn, the Helios platform will leverage UALink 
for scale-up interconnects, while adopting Ethernet for scale-out connectivity 
(implementing UALink over Ethernet).

Aspeed (5274 TT, TWD18,265, Buy [1]) 
• At Computex 2026, Aspeed's showcase highlighted its evolution from a pure Baseboard 
Management Controller (BMC) supplier toward a broader platform management and

infrastructure control provider. The company demonstrated solutions spanning BMC, 
Satellite Management Controller (SMC, or mini-BMC), I/O expansion, and security IC. 
• A key emphasis at Aspeed's booth was the company's adoption of RISC-V architecture 
and OCP open-source frameworks to support Caliptra — an IP designed to execute 
root-of-trust functions. This security enhancement is expected to be implemented across 
the majority of Aspeed's product lines, thereby elevating the overall security standard of 
its devices. 
• Another significant highlight was the introduction of the AST1840, succeeding the 
previous AST1800. The AST1700/1800/1840 series constitutes Aspeed's I/O expander 
product line, with the AST1800 marking the first edition to incorporate an embedded 
FPGA within the I/O expander. With the AST1840, Aspeed has transitioned to 
outsourcing the FPGA chip from Lattice Semiconductor, resulting in the development of 
a new System-on-Chip (SoC) for the AST1840. 
• Regarding the near-term outlook, following the company's revenue guidance of 
TWD4.1-4.3 billion, we anticipate an additional revenue step-up from 4Q26, as Aspeed 
has qualified with a second OSAT partner, enabling broader support from substrate 
vendors. With respect to the T-glass to E-glass substrate certification, we expect the 
transition to be completed successfully; however, the initial production batch of the 
AST2700 will incorporate substrates containing both T-glass and E-glass. Over the 
longer term, only the E-glass substrate version is expected to remain in production. 
• The company expressed a continued positive outlook on CPU server demand, driven by 
the growth of agentic AI workloads. According to Aspeed's latest forecast, BMC unit 
shipments are projected to reach approximately 8.5 million units in 2026. Looking ahead 
to 2027, we think BMC shipments from the conventional server segment will grow by at 
least 30% year-over-year.

Delta Electronics (2308 TT, TWD2,425, Buy [1]) 
• Delta’s Computex 2026 showcase its strategy of extending from power and thermal 
components into broader AI datacentre infrastructure. Delta highlighted five main 
exhibition areas: modular datacentre, micro grid solutions, HVDC solutions, Vera Rubin 
solutions, and Edge AI. We believe the key investment implication is that Delta is 
positioning itself not only as a supplier of power supplies and cooling components, but 
also as an infrastructure partner spanning power conversion. 
• 800VDC remains the core product direction. Delta’s key AI datacentre message is built 
around 800VDC architecture. Its showcase included an 800VDC in-row power system, 
90kW DC-DC power shelf, 110kW AC-DC power shelf, 12kW power distribution board, 
and DC-DC conversion modules. The 800VDC in-row power system is designed to 
simplify the power conversion architecture and achieve over 98% system efficiency, 
while also integrating 480kW of total BBU for short-duration support to critical loads. We 
see this as the next step in Delta’s power roadmap as AI rack power emerge. 
• Micro-grid is becoming the next key question from investors on top of HVDC, while SST 
and SOFC becomes the key discussion. However, these new growth drivers likely won’t 
bear fruit in just 1-2 years. The microgrid solution integrates renewables, energy 
storage, fuel cells and other power sources to support datacentre operation in grid-
connected and off-grid modes. Delta’s solid-state transformer (SST) can directly convert 
medium-voltage AC to low-voltage 800VDC, with high power density and modular 
design to reduce footprint and power losses. The SOFC fuel cell offers 60% generation 
efficiency and 24-hour stable power supply with modular expansion. We see SST and 
SOFC as strategic long-term options tied to AI datacentre power trend, while the nearer-
term investment focus remains 800VDC power racks, power shelves, distribution 
boards, BBU, and liquid cooling.

### Page 3

Taiwan Datacentre Hardware 
4 June 2026 
3

Hon Hai (2317 TT, TWD293.0, Buy [1]) 
• As one of NVIDIA's key strategic partners, Hon Hai presented its comprehensive lineup 
of NVIDIA AI servers at Computex. Notably, the Vera CPU rack and CPO switch were 
absent from the exhibition. Hon Hai's demonstration included new computing trays and 
switches for the VR200, as well as the HGX NVL8 — a new HGX configuration featuring 
eight GPUs, dual x86 CPUs, and integrated liquid-cooling components. The compact 
design of the NVL8 enables a reduced form factor of 2U (with a 1U height of 45mm). 
• In addition, Hon Hai showcased its Co-Packaging Connector (CPC), designed for 
integration into switches such as the Spectrum-X. Based on our understanding, Hon 
Hai/FIT is capable of delivering 224 Gbps per lane, achieving an aggregate throughput 
of 12.8 Tbps per connector. We believe that at least two clients are currently evaluating 
the adoption of this CPC technology.

• From FIT's perspective, the company also presented its Paladin connector, intended for 
use within the mid-plane of the computing tray. This connector, along with the mid-plane, 
is expected to serve as a key replacement for Amphenol's flyover cable. We believe FIT 
is actively developing a next-generation connector solution to be deployed within the 
Kyber architecture, where the mid-plane will serve as the interconnect between both the 
computing and switch trays.

Chenbro (8210 TT, TWD1,420, Buy [1]) 
• As an established rack vendor on NVIDIA's Reference Vendor List (RVL), Chenbro 
showcased several rack solutions compatible with NVIDIA's VR NVL72 at the exhibition. 
• We understand that Chenbro is currently engaged in multiple new rack projects, 
encompassing noise-cancellation chassis, IT racks for NVIDIA/AMD, liquid-cooling racks 
(including CDU racks in both Liquid-to-Liquid and Liquid-to-Air configurations), and 
switch racks. This strategic expansion underscores Chenbro's transition from a chassis 
vendor to a full-scale rack vendor, reflecting the broader industry shift whereby end 
customers are increasingly designing their infrastructure at the rack level rather than the 
chassis level. 
• We anticipate that the margin profile for rack solutions will reach c.25-30%, compared to 
our previous estimate of c.15-20%, driven by the significant degree of customization 
involved. Consequently, our revised long-term gross margin forecast has been lifted 
from 25-26% to 28-29%.  
• During the booth tour, Chenbro emphasized its enhanced development cycle, which is 
designed to further reduce time-to-market for its clients. The company also highlighted 
its diversified development teams - comprising software engineers, industrial designers, 
mechanical engineers, and other specialists - to provide comprehensive support to end 
customers. We believe Chenbro's role is a key co-design and the most fast partner for 
its end customers, making it a critical differentiator from its competitors and mitigating 
the impact of competitive pressures.

We remain positive on Gigabyte’s AI server business and expect a
39% CAGR for the company’s AI server revenues in 2025-28E, with

AI servers revenue contribution up to 74% in 2028E (vs. 54% in
2025). The company’s April revenues were up 74% YoY, or 51%
higher than our previous estimates on AI server ramp-up, and we
expect the strong momentum to continue, driven by (1) rack-level AI
server specifica
tion upgrade to drive dollar content upgrade, (2)
server capacity expansion to support volume ramp-up, and (3) AI
total solution helping clients to deploy AI with higher efficienc

y.

AI server business ramp-up: The company is capable of developing
AI servers across differ ent architecture, with GB300 NVL72 in
production, and Vera Rubin platforms under development.
Meanwhile, Gigabyte also provides integrated solution incl. the
pre-integrated AI Pod (GIGAPOD) for high-density GPU cluster
deployment, and the united GPM platform on monitoring,
scheduling, and resource management. We see the company’s total

solution helps to a
ccelerate clients’ AI deployment. We expect to see
AI server business growth with specifica tion upgrade towards new
platform and capacity ramp up.

74% YoY/ 34% MoM to NT$52.3bn, or 51% beat our previous

the company’s 2Q26E revenue to grow at 26% YoY (or 22% MoM) to

pull-in. We expect the rising AI server revenue contribution to
support growth ahead.

Earnings revision: We raise NI by 36%/ 26%/ 29% in 2026-28E on higher revenues,
reflec ting our positive view on (1) the company’s capabilities to deliver AI server across
various architecture, benefiting fr om the dollar content upgrade, (2) capacity ramp-up
to support volume ramp-up, and (3) comprehensive ecosystem: offering the
pre-integrated AI Pod (GIGAPOD) to enhance the efficienc y of high-density GPU cluster
deployment. We raise 2026 GM on mix upgrade towards high-end solution and cost

management, while lower 2027/ 28E GM by 0.2ppts/ 0.2ppts to reflec t higher revenue
contribution from rack-level server with lower margin.
GS vs. consensus: Our earnings estimates in 2026/ 27E are largely in line with
consensus. Our 2026E revenue estimate is 3% lower than consensus due to lower
revenues of graphic card and motherboard, while our revenue estimate in 2027E is 1%
higher than consensus on positive outlook of AI server revenues, with lower GM due to

lower margin of rack AI server business.

### Page 5

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is
based on a 16.8x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share
performance.
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3)
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Asus (2357.TW): Enhanced AI-driven PC for LLM workloads and local AI
tasks; TP raised to NT$1,082; Neutral
6 June 2026 | 6:05PM HKT
ASUS showcased the new ProArt P16/ P14 based on the NVIDIA RTX Spark
Superchip (Link) during the Computex 2026, highlighting the faster AI model loading,
ultra-responsive multitasking, and efficient pr ocessing for creators, including the
local AI tasks. Despite near-term pressures of memory cost weighing on the PC end

demand, we see the company still benefits fr om the rising AI PC adoption rate and
specifica tion upgrade for enhanced AI user experiences. Meanwhile, we expect to
see company’s AI server business ramp up across various platforms, supported by
comprehensive offerings and client div ersification. Maintain Neutral with TP raised to
NT$1,082.
Monthly revenues preview: ASUS April revenues were +46% YoY/ -5% MoM to
NT$82bn, or 36% higher than our estimates, and we attribute it to product mix

upgrade with spec upgrade and customer pull-in. Looking ahead, despite memory
cost pressures remaining, we expect the mix upgrade towards AI PC and rack-level AI
server ramp up to support 2Q/ 3Q26E growth at 26%/ 12% YoY. We model May/ Jun
revenues to grow 26%/ 9% YoY to NT$79bn/ NT$75bn, while MoM decline on the
high base.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Aspeed Technology  | Asia Pacific
Substrate Constraints Remain
the Key Uncertainty, Adding an

OW
What's new: Aspeed reported May revenue of NT$1,282mn, +0.25% MoM and
+69% Y/Y, slightly lower than our expectation of NT$1,381mn, but we think the
shortfall is likely to be recovered in June after Aspeed receives more substrate
support.
Stay OW
: Demand remains very strong in the near term, while Aspeed's upside is

capped by limited T-glass supply. However, as Aspeed is finishing up the E-glass
qualification, we do expect revenue to start climbing in 2H26. Also, Aspeed has
qualified another Taiwanese substrate vendor to help secure sufficient supply in 2H.
We still expect margin to reach 70% after a potential second round of price hike in
4Q26 (see earnings result pulse
). The stock is currently trading at 53x 2027e EPS.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Update
Chenbro  | Asia Pacific
May Sales +22% MoM/+37%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales were NT$2,517mn (+22% MoM/+37% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 60% of our 2Q26 estimate at NT$7,596mn (+7% QoQ/
+40% YoY) and 59% of consensus at NT$7,736mn (+9% QoQ/+42% YoY).
Our view:
• 2Q26 revenue is tracking largely in line with our expectations.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook, supported by GPU/

ASIC and general purpose servers, as well as increasing rack shipments.
• Chenbro is also tapping into more rack-level mechanical components and
integration services, by leveraging its expertise in the chassis business.
• We maintain the view that Chenbro will benefit from AI server shipment
growth and its share gains, as well as the incremental contribution from rack
offerings in the coming years.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.9. This is in line the average of
0.9 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 14% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

May Sales Up 30.2% YoY but

Details:
• Revenue was NT$1,629mn in May, up 30.2%  YoY but down 29.6% MoM.

• The company expects June revenue to remain weak and July demand to  see a
strong increase as optical lenses enter mass production.
Our view:
• We believe Android smartphone shipments will face headwinds in 2026,
which could negatively affect lens demand.

• Besides smartphone lenses, the company plans to spend great efforts on
optical communication products. If progress is smooth, they could become
new growth drivers in 2027-28.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Genius Electronic Optical Co. Ltd. (3406.TW)
Base case, residual income model, which is the same method we use for our Greater China Tech
coverage. We apply a CoE of 8.5% (3% risk free, 1.0 beta and 5.5% ERP), a medium-term net
profit CAGR of 4% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n More share gains in iPhones
n Better-than-expected mixed reality demand from Apple
n Better-than-expected VR/MR shipments

Risks to Downside
n Delays in Apple's mixed reality shipments
n Share loss in iPhones
n Execution/yield
n Slower VR/MR shipments

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Taiwan Technology: TSMT (6278.TW, NC): Mgmt. Call takeaways: SMT
solution expanding to AI optical module from consumer electronics
10 June 2026 | 10:52PM HKT
We talked to TSMT (Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology, 6278.TW, Not Covered)
management recently. TSMT is a global leading SMT (surface-mount technology)
solution provider that serves leading players in consumer electronics, automobile, AI
server ODM, optical networking, satellite, robotics, etc. Management highlights the

growth opportunities in AI infrastructures, such as optical modules and AI servers.
Management expects a rising contribution from optical module SMT business in
coming years, which carries a higher GM than consumer electronics, driving the
company’s earnings growth.
Read across to AI server supply chain: Management’s positive tone on AI
infrastructure supporting TSMT’s growth echoes our positive view on AI server
supply chain. We expect the global cloud capex uptrend and continuous AI

application development to support AI infrastructure end demand, and the
customization / diversifica tion trend (e.g. HGX, ASIC, AI servers powered by multiple
chipset suppliers) to continue, benefiting AI ser ver supply chain. Buy: Hon Hai,
Wiwynn, Mitac, Wistron, Lenovo.
Company profile: Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (6278.TW, Not Covered) is a
global leading LCD SMT solution provider, providing material procurement, process
engineering design, SMT, quality assurance, etc. The company has a global layout,

with production sites in Taiwan, mainland China, India, Mexico, and Vietnam, and
serves leading players in consumer electronics, automobile, AI server ODM, optical
networking, satellite, etc.
Key takeaways: Management remains positive on growth ahead: (1) existing
business (consumer electronics SMT) to be supported by its major customer’s (a
US-based global leading brand) new notebook model, for which TSMT remains the
major supplier, (2) new business (AI infrastructure): TSMT penetrates a US-based

global leading optical module suppliers, and targets to further expand customer
base in optical modules. Customers’ products migration toward 1.6T optical modules
also drives TSMT’s growth ahead. The SMT for optical module also carries a higher
GM than consumer electronics, per management, supporting the company’s
profitability impr
ovement. AI servers is also another growing opportunity for the
company, per management, riding on the strong end demand growth driven by the

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
May sales down 2% m/m, but
up 22% Y/Y
Our view:
• Unaudited April + May sales are tracking slightly behind our and consensus
estimates for 2Q26, primarily due to slower PC and consumer electronics
shipments.
• May notebook shipments came in 5% below our estimate at 2.0M units
(+11% m/m, -17% y/y).
• Compal's guidance continues to be that its notebook shipments will grow in
the low-single-digits q/q and revenue will grow in the double-digits q/q in

2Q26.
• We  believe the continued pull-in of consumer electronic devices in 1H26 will
lead to a more sub-seasonal 2H26.
• On our estimates, Compal is trading at ~17x 2027e P/E, above its past five-
year P/E average of ~14x.
• We stay UW on Compal.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Compal Electronics (2324.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.1% (beta
1.3, equity risk premium 6.0%, and risk-free rate 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%,
and a long-term earnings growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB and smartphone demand
n Further share gains in iPad
n Margin expansion thanks to better scale

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Margin contraction from sales shortfall and fierce price competition
n Weaker-than-expected iPad shipments

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

2610.TT 華航 
 客貨穩定，高油價影響獲利

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
中華航空目前共有 68 架客機、18 架貨機，客機目前共飛航包含亞、歐、美及大洋洲
29 個國家、191 個客運航點；貨機飛航包含亞、歐、美洲共 15 個國家、33 個航點。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 21 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，20 
【大盤指數/股價】46459 / 19.95

➢ 1Q26 EPS 0.85 元，獲利優於預期 
華航 1Q26 營收 569.97 億元，QoQ+5.23%，YoY+8.01%，稅後淨利
51.69 億元，QoQ+39.58%，YoY+23.51%，EPS 0.85 元。其中，客運
營收 291.75 億元，YoY+7.25%，平均載客率 84.8%，較 2025 年增加
5.1 個百分點；貨運營收164.62 億元，YoY+5.31%。華航 1Q26 燃油均
價 85.4 美元/桶，YoY-6.1%，燃油佔營運成本比重維持在 30%。 
➢ 2Q26 航空燃油成本大增侵蝕獲利 
中油 2Q26 航空燃油牌價平均 190 美元/桶，較 1Q26 平均 94 美元/桶
上漲 102%，考量油料避險合約及用油遞延，投研部預估華航 2Q26 航
空燃油成本平均 160 美元/桶，QoQ+87.3%， 預估華航2Q26 燃油成本
將較 1Q26 增加 100 億元左右。投研部預估華航 2Q26 營收 620.53 億
元，QoQ+8.87%，YoY+20.88%， 稅後淨利7.30 億元 ，QoQ-85.87%，
YoY-81.06%，EPS 0.13 元。 
➢ 2026 年客貨量穩定成長，油價主導獲利變化 
美伊至今尚未達成和談協議 ， 隨著替代能源及其他產油國進口補足缺口 ，
市場供需緊繃緩解，原油價格及煉油利差逐漸收斂。然美伊和談進展比
預期慢，荷姆茲海峽封閉時間比預期長，油價仍維持在95 美元之上，因
此投研部上修 2026 年航空燃油預估值至 131 美元/桶，相較華航 2025
年燃油均價 86.7 美元/桶，上漲51%， 若以一年用油量1,600~1,610 萬
桶計算，華航 2026 年燃油成本將較 2025 年增加 200~230 億元。 
➢ 預估華航 2026 年 EPS 1.91 元，建議區間操作，目標價 21 元 
投研部預估華航 2026 年營收 2,402.32 億元，YoY+14.87%，稅後淨利
110.51 億元，YoY-24.83%，EPS 1.91 元；預估 2027 年營收 2,268.86
億元，YoY-5.56%，稅後淨利 114.56 億元，YoY+3.67%，EPS 2.06 元。
航空客貨量需求穩定，但中東戰事及油價不確定性仍高，燃油成本壓力
大，華航目前 PB 1.2X，位於歷史 PB 區間 0.9~1.5X 中間位置，維持區
間操作的投資建議，目標價 21 元(2026F PB 1.25X)。

2610 華航 加權指數

### Page 2

個股報告

公司簡介 
中華航空成立於 1959 年，是國內貨運規模最大的航空公司，目前華航共有 68 架客
機、18 架貨機，客機目前共飛航包含亞、歐、美及大洋洲 29 個國家、191 個客運航
點；貨機飛航包含亞、歐、美洲共 15 個國家、33 個航點，每週平均營運班次約 106
班。華航 1Q26 客運佔營收比重 61.1%，貨運佔 34.4%。因應客貨運市場的持續蓬勃
發展，並進一步提升整體航機的服務效能，中華航空全力推動機隊汰舊換新與航網擴
張計劃，預計自 2029 年起，陸續引進 15 架空中巴士 A350-1000 客機、15 架波音
777-9 客機及 8 架 777-8F 貨機，此批最新世代的飛機，將與先前已規劃引進的波音 
787 新機協同合作，組成全面升級的嶄新客貨運主力機隊，投入各大核心航線服務。 
圖 1：華航營收比重

資料來源：華航，國票投顧 
1Q26 EPS 0.85 元，獲利優於預期 
華航 1Q26 營收 569.97 億元，QoQ+5.23%，YoY+8.01%，稅後淨利 51.69 億元，
QoQ+39.58%，YoY+23.51%，EPS 0.85 元 。 客運方面 ，1Q26 適逢農曆春節及寒假 ，
出國旅遊需求強勁 ， 客運搭載人數312.1 萬人 ，YoY+7.52%， 客運營收291.75 億元 ，
YoY+7.25%，平均載客率 84.8%，較 2025 年增加 5.1 個百分點。貨運方面，受惠AI
伺服器及半導體產品穩定出口，1Q26 貨運營收 164.62 億元，YoY+5.31%，不過由
於 2025 年因關稅提前出貨，運價基期相對高，1Q26 單位收益 YoY 衰退 2.13%。成
本方面，華航1Q26 燃油均價 85.4 美元/桶，YoY-6.1%，燃油佔營運成本比重維持在
30%。

2Q26 航空燃油成本大增侵蝕獲利 
5 月國際原油價格下跌、煉油利差收斂，中油調降 6 月的國際航線航空燃油牌價至
160.1 美元/桶 ， 較5 月 208.7 美元/桶下跌 23.2%，2Q26 平均 190 美元/桶，較1Q26
平均 94 美元/桶上漲 102%，考量油料避險合約及用油遞延，投研部預估華航 2Q26
航空燃油成本平均 160 美元/桶，QoQ+87.3%， 預估華航2Q26 燃油成本將較 1Q26
增加 100 億元左右。投研部預估華航 2Q26 營收 620.53 億元，QoQ+8.87%，
YoY+20.88%，稅後淨利 7.30 億元，QoQ-85.87%，YoY-81.06%，EPS 0.13 元。 
2026 年客貨運量需求穩健，油價主導獲利表現 
展望 2026 年，IMF 預估全球經濟成長率約 3.1%，亞太地區表現優於全球平均，在經
濟溫和成長及半導體設備、AI 投資浪潮下，全球客貨量維持穩定成長的態勢，國際航
協(IATA)預估 2026 年全球客運量成長 4.9%，貨運量成長 2.6%。 
2026 年主導航空業獲利最大關鍵仍在油價。美伊至今尚未達成和談協議，隨著替代
能源及其他產油國進口補足缺口 ， 市場供需緊繃緩解 ， 原油價格及煉油利差逐漸收斂 。
然美伊和談進展比預期慢，荷姆茲海峽封閉時間比預期長，油價仍維持在 95 美元之
上 ，因此投研部上修2026 年航空燃油預估值至 131 美元/桶 ，相較華航2025 年燃油
均價 86.7 美元/桶，上漲 51%，若以一年用油量 1,600~1,610 萬桶計算，華航 2026
年燃油成本將較 2025 年增加 200~230 億元，而燃油附加費僅能反映部分新增燃油
成本，預估華航 2026 年獲利衰退 24%。 
預估華航 2026 年 EPS 1.91 元，建議區間操作，目標價 21 元 
投研部預估華航 2026 年營收 2,402.32 億元 ，YoY+14.87%， 稅後淨利110.51 億元 ，
YoY-24.83%，EPS 1.91 元；預估 2027 年營收 2,268.86 億元，YoY-5.56%，稅後淨
利 114.56 億元，YoY+3.67%，EPS 2.0 6 元。2026 年航空客貨量需求穩定，但中東
戰事及油價不確定性仍高，燃油成本壓力大，華航目前 PB 1.2X ，位於歷史 PB 區間
0.9~1.5X 中間位置，維持區間操作的投資建議，目標價 21 元(2026F PB 1.25X)。

### Page 4

大立光 (3008 TT)  Buy

展出 FAU 與相關零組件，給予 Buy 投資建議。  
投資建議 ： iPhone 鏡頭出貨進入傳統淡季，預期 06/2026 營收
將為近期最低點，後續將進入 iPhone 鏡頭出貨旺季，且近期展
出高通道數 FAU 及其相關零組件，顯示公司對此業務表示高度
信心，有助於提升本益比，故給予 Buy 投資建議。  
進入全年營運最低點 ， 預估2Q26 營收 146.67 億元 ，QoQ-5.64% ，
EPS41.89 元：展望 2Q26 ， iPhone17 系列機種已進入銷售生命
週期尾聲，鏡頭將進入全年最淡一季；中國手機大客戶發表與
Apple 摺疊機尺寸相同的摺疊機以及強調拍照效果的高階機種 ，
對高階鏡頭需求增加，減緩 iPhone 鏡頭出貨下滑的幅度，預估
2Q26 營收 146.67 億元， QoQ-5.64% ，稅後淨利 54.79 億元，
QoQ-10.53% ， EPS41.89 元。  
COMPUTEX 中展出 PMLA 與多通道光纖陣列，展現切入 FAU
信心： 公司展出用於 CPO 產業鏈的新產品，第一款是 PMLA ，
另一個產品則是多通道光纖陣列， PMLA 是 FAU 的關鍵零組件
之一，主要用於改善光纖與 PIC 之間的光耦合效率以及雷射光
準直性能，多通道光纖陣列則用於固定光纖，用於 CPO 傳輸的
單模光纖芯直徑僅有 9 微米，因此 FAU 的精度要求極高，誤差
不得超過 0.3 微米，若把多通道光纖陣列結合 PMLA 即構成
FAU ，會展中亦展出 40 芯光纖的產品。此次展出 FAU 及相關
零組件，對一向保守穩健經營的大立光而言，不啻為對自家產
品充滿信心的表現。  
iPhone 鏡頭規格持續升級，預估 2026 年營收 676.10 億元，
YoY+10.57% ，EPS191.07 元：展望 2026 年， Apple 摺疊機發售
可望抵銷 iPhone 下滑的銷量 ， 帶動整體iPhone 銷量持平 ；iPhone
半導體零組件多為自製 ， iPhone 抵抗成本上揚能力優於 Android
手機，故能依照原定計畫，升級高階機種主鏡頭為可變光圈鏡
頭，可變光圈鏡頭因為需要多段光圈皆能精準對焦，需要增加
檢測站數導致良率下降，因此鏡頭價格較高，預估 2026 年營收
676.10 億元 ，YoY+10.57% ，稅 後 淨 利249.93 億元 ，YoY+17.47% ，
EPS191.07 元。目前大立光已展出高通道數 FAU 展現信心，未
來 FAU 業務將成為影響本益比高低的重要因素。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 9 日

2 群益投顧 
  大立光 ESG ：  
公司響應溫室效應氣體排放減量管理，以達到二氧化碳減量目的。 2021 年
持續透過各項改善節能提報及廠區綠化，新建廠規劃建置 1,100KW 太陽光
電，期望有效控制碳排放量，於 2050 年前同步政府政策使用再生能源、降
低使用電力所間接排放的二氧化碳減量，實現環境永續的未來。為達 2050 
年使用再生能源政策，大立光能源結構開始轉型，提高再生能源配比如太陽
能及風力發電等比重。  
進入全年營運最低點，預估 2Q26 營收 146.67 億元， QoQ-5.64% ，EPS41.89
元：  
展望 2Q26 ， iPhone17 系列機種已進入銷售生命週期尾聲，鏡頭將進入全年
最淡一季；中國手機大客戶發表與 Apple 摺疊機尺寸相同的摺疊機以及強
調拍照效果的高階機種，對高階鏡頭需求增加，減緩 iPhone 鏡頭出貨下滑
的幅度。  
在 iPhone 陣營方面，雖然 iPhone17e 仍在銷售，但因 iPhone17 系列機種已
進入銷售生命週期尾聲，鏡頭將進入全年最淡一季，預期 06/2026 是全季低
點，但後續隨著 iPhone18 鏡頭陸續出貨，帶動營運進入旺季。  
在 Android 陣營方面，因中國手機大客戶發表與 Apple 摺疊機尺寸相同的摺
疊機以及強調拍照效果的高階機種，對高階鏡頭需求增加，減緩 iPhone 鏡
頭出貨下滑的幅度。  
進入全年營運最低點，預估 2Q26 營收 146.67 億元， QoQ-5.64% 。  
雖然中國手機客戶將拉貨較多鏡頭，但因營運規模較小，估計毛利率約略持
平，預估稅後淨利 54.79 億元， QoQ-10.53% ， EPS41.89 元。  
COMPUTEX 中展出 PMLA 與多通道光纖陣列，展現切入 FAU 信心：  
大立光在 2026 年 COMPUTEX 會展中展出用於 CPO 產業鏈的新產品，第一
款是多通道微透鏡陣列 (Parallel Micro Lens Array, PMLA) ，另一個產品則是
多通道光纖陣列 (Multi-Row Fiber Array) ， PMLA 是 FAU 的關鍵零組件之
一，主要用於改善光纖與光子晶片 (Photonics Integrated Circuit, PIC) 之間的
光耦合效率以及雷射光準直 (Collimation) 性能，降低光耦合損失並提升光學
傳輸品質，多通道光纖陣列則用於固定光纖，由於用於 CPO 傳輸的單模光
纖芯直徑僅有 9 微米 (Micrometer) ，因此 FAU 相關產品的精度要求極高，誤
差不得超過 0.3 微米，否則將會造成過大的光耦合損失，光訊號傳輸將會失
效；若把多通道光纖陣列結合 PMLA 即構成 FAU，本次會展中亦展出 40 芯
光纖的產品。  
大立光自從事手機鏡頭事業時期，就已具有設計自動化設備的豐富經驗，本
次展出 FAU 相關產品，並表示將對客戶送樣與設計自動化機台，預計 1~2
年內量產 FAU ，此量產時程恰巧與美系大廠 Scale-Up CPO 放量時點高度重
合，此次展出 FAU 及相關零組件，對一向保守穩健經營的大立光而言，不
啻為對自家產品充滿信心的表現。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 9 日

3 群益投顧 
圖一、 PMLA                    圖二、多通道光纖陣列

iPhone 鏡頭規格持續升級，預估 2026 年營收 676.10 億元， YoY+10.57% ，
EPS191.07 元：  
展望 2026 年， Apple 摺疊機發售可望抵銷 iPhone 下滑的銷量，帶動整體
iPhone 銷量持平； iPhone 半導體零組件多為自製， iPhone 抵抗成本上揚能
力優於 Android 手機，故能依照原定計畫升級高階機種主鏡頭為可變光圈鏡
頭，可變光圈鏡頭因為需要多段光圈皆能精準對焦，而增加檢測站數將導致
良率下降，因此鏡頭價格較高。  
預期 2026 年開賣的 iPhone18 機型當中， iPhone18 標準版將延後至 2027 年
開賣， 3Q26 有 iPhone18 Pro 與 iPhone18 Pro Max 開賣，也將發表新型的折
疊機款，雖然記憶體與 NAND Flash 價格上漲，但因高價值半導體零組件為
自行設計，例如：處理器、無線連網晶片、 Cellular Modem 、高頻定位晶片
等 … ，硬體成本競爭力優於 Android 手機同業， iPhone18 系列可望不漲價，
加上摺疊機將生產千萬部以上，摺疊機發售可望彌補 iPhone18 標準版的空
缺，預期整體 iPhone 銷量持平。 iPhone 因抵抗成本能力較強，依照原定計
畫升級高階機種主鏡頭為可變光圈鏡頭。可變光圈鏡頭因為需要多段光圈
能夠精準對焦，故在生產過程需要設立多個檢測站點，由於檢測越多次就會
挑出更多不良品，導致鏡頭良率較低，因此鏡頭價格較高。  
隨著 AI 推論應用需求逐漸放大，吸納大量儲存資源， DRAM 、 NAND Flash
因短期需求大幅增加導致價格大漲，推升手機成本向上， Android 手機陣營
業者半導體零組件多為外購， Android 手機抵抗成本上揚能力弱於 iPhone ，
已有部分 Android 手機廠商因記憶體成本大漲，凍結 2026 年手機鏡頭升級
計畫而沿用舊鏡頭，並提高手機價格的決策恐將壓抑銷量，預期 Android 手
機鏡頭營收貢獻將下滑。  
iPhone 鏡頭持續升級，預估 2026 年營收 676.10 億元， YoY+10.57% ，稅後
淨利 249.93 億元， YoY+17.47% ， EPS191.07 元。  
近期 AI 算力基礎建設持續往高速傳輸發展，單通道 400Gbps 已逼近銅線傳
輸極限， Nvidia 在 GTC 2026 大會上已揭露下一代 NV Link Scale-up 將採用
光銅並進傳輸的技術路線， CPO 應用範圍將由 Scale-Out 擴大至 Scale-up ，

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 6 月 9 日

4 群益投顧 
第一階段的機櫃間 Scale-up CPO 將自 2H27 量產，由供應鏈訪查的訊息來
看 ， 機櫃間Scale-up CPO 可望有 10 萬台的出貨量 ， 第二階段的機櫃內 Scale-
up CPO 將從 2028 年放量，出貨量更是機櫃間 Scale-up CPO 倍數以上的量，
故連接光電轉換引擎與光纖的高通道 (High Channel) 數 FAU(Fiber 
Assembling Unit, FAU) 用量也隨之提升，預期將顯著推升 CPO 相關零組件
出貨動能，目前大立光已展出高通道數 FAU 以及相關零組件，未來 FAU 業
務將成為影響本益比高低的重要因素。  
投資建議：  
iPhone 鏡頭出貨進入傳統淡季，預期 06/2026 營收將為近期最低點，後續將
進入 iPhone 鏡頭出貨旺季，且近期展出高通道數 FAU 及其相關零組件，顯
示公司對此業務表示高度信心，有助於提升本益比，故給予 Buy 投資建議。

  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

麗清 (3346 TT)  Buy

切入機器人供應鏈，投資建議維持 Buy，調升目標價。  
投資建議 Buy：雖 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣，但考量公司車
燈產品後續在手訂單提升，且切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈，有
利於公司長期發展與評價提升，投資建議維持 Buy ，目標價調
高至 28 元 (以 PB 0.95 倍評價 )。  
麗清切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈 ：麗清近期宣布切入機器人產
業。公司藉由在車用領域累積的車規級模組設計與 PCBA 製造
能力，以技術同源的優勢切入敏實集團的機器人與機器狗供應
鏈。首批產品預計於 06/2026 開始小量出貨。麗清開發的核心
關節控制模組將應用於人形機器人的 26 個大關節，以協助完成
複雜肢體運動與精準控制；同時也導入四足機器狗的 12 個關鍵
關節，以滿足高頻動態反應與多環境適應需求。在供應鏈佈局
上，麗清與敏實集團及旗下關鍵零組件廠綠的諧波結盟，由麗
清生產模組交由敏實組裝整合，最終出貨予中國人形機器人指
標廠智元機器人。此機器人產品線的毛利率推估落在
15%~20% ，2027 年為量產元年，預估訂單首年銷售額約 1.79 億
元人民幣，後續將隨著機器人市場需求提升而逐步放大。  
預估 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣： 1Q26 營收 16.94 億元，QoQ-
23.04% 、YoY-17.32% 。因受中國市場內捲影響且營收規模下滑，
毛利率下降至 8.89% ，因營業費用處於高檔，本業、整體營運陷
入虧損， EPS -0.67 元。 2Q26 方面， 04/2026 營收 MoM 已小幅
回升， 05/2026 、 06/2026 營收有望維持穩定。預估 2Q26 營收
20.64 億元， QoQ+21.85% 、YoY-9.04% ，因營收規模相對回升且
產品組合較佳，毛利率有望回升至 11.45% ，營業利益 0.16 億
元。 2Q26 整體營運小幅獲利， EPS 0.08 元。 2026 年方面，北美
車燈控制器出貨維持穩定，而中國市場持續內捲但狀況有機會
改善。預估營收 81.83 億元， YoY-4.21% 。毛利率於 1Q26 落底
後， 2Q26 有望回升，預估 2026 年毛利率較 2025 年小幅提升，
若公司 2Q26~3Q26 費用管控得宜，預估本業、整體營運表現將
落於損平邊緣， EPS -0.25 元。

  麗清 (3346 TT) ESG ：  
溫室氣體排放： (1) 對於因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略，以減排、綠能
生產及環保達到永續經營目標。並以生產製程減少廢棄物產生、優先採購對
環境傷害最少之原物料、管控耗能設備及依操作許可從嚴格處理生產過程
所產生之廢棄物。 (2) 溫室氣體排放量減量目標，在節能方面，透過持續的監
控系統及生產單位自主管理，使生產單位的電力消耗日益降低，達到能源的
有效利用，以年整體用電密集度下降 5%及年整體排碳密集度下降 5%為目
標。(3) 溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫， 1.更新生產機器及使用採集中生
產原則。優先新機台生產，擬裝置節能變頻模組為優先，以較高效率的產能
先排產，再使用次舊機台，以節約電力消耗。機台開動以預計產量排滿為主，
提高稼動率，集中在較高效率的機台生產，減少低效率生產，減少溫室氣體
排放。 2.辦公室節能政策，室內空調冷氣以 26°C 為原則。午休時段用餐後
即關燈，下班後則分區關閉電源。 (4) 產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳
效果， 1.持續尋找新綠能材料或能源機會。 2.對內外單位儘可能以電子表單
寄送，減少紙張消耗。  
專業 LED 車燈模組供應商：  
麗清 (3346 TT) 為專業 LED 車燈模組供應商，除了量產 OEM LED 車燈模組
產品，也有能力為客戶設計打造 ODM 車燈模組產品。麗清為中國最大 LED
車燈模組廠，市占率接近三成。麗清車燈模組供應整車前裝市場，客戶包括
整車廠及車燈廠。以主要客戶占 2025 年營收比重來看，華域視覺 (車燈模組
與控制器 )占 34%、 HASCO( 控制器，華域視覺關係企業 )占 24%、海拉 (車燈
模組 )占 12% 、廣州小糸 (車燈模組，日系車為主 )占 9%、大茂 (車燈模組 )占
5%、 MAGNA( 車燈模組 )占 5%、 ZKW( 車燈模組，韓系 )占 3%、其他占 8%。
產品組合方面，車燈控制器 (包含出貨至美國及中國 )占 2025 年營收比重
29%、尾燈占 39%、頭燈及日行燈 (兩個會設計在一起 )占 31%、其他占 1%。
